Measured: overpass-api.de answered a 1° tile in ~20 s / 20–30 MB raw (the same query took 201 s the night before on kumi), a tile compacts to 0.7–2 MB; N49E038 keeps 1 685 built-up / 1 832 forest / 244 water / 98 rail polygons after dropping 21 299 tiny ones. **Serving**: `ukraineBaseProxy` in `vite.config.js` also answers `areas/meta` and `areas/<key>` (key validated by regex, same gzip + ETag path as the other snapshot files). **Client**: `src/data/ukraineAreas.js` (pure: `pickAreaTiles` — tiles intersecting the camera's view rectangle, nearest to its centre first, max 6; `areasWanted` below 420 km; `filterAreasForDraw` with stricter draw thresholds built ≥ 0.02 km², forest ≥ 0.08, water ≥ 0.03 and caps 2 500/2 000/800/1 500 per tile; `AREAS_STYLE`), `src/data/ukraineAreasLayer.js` (`createUkraineAreasLayer`: loads `areas/meta` on show, recomputes on `camera.moveEnd` (350 ms settle) and on map-stack change, fetches missing tiles, shows at most 6, keeps 12 in memory with LRU eviction, hides everything above 420 km and whenever the globe is hidden (`isGlobeHiddenForStack` — Google 3D would otherwise get grey polygons draped over real buildings); each tile = up to four batched primitives in `scene.groundPrimitives` with `allowPicking: false` and `classificationType: BOTH`: water and built-up as `GroundPrimitive` + `PerInstanceColorAppearance`, forests as `GroundPrimitive` + `MaterialAppearance` with the registered Fabric material `OkoForestDots` (white dots in SCREEN pixels via `gl_FragCoord`, spacing 7 px — the density does not change with zoom, exactly the Rybar texture), railways as `GroundPolylinePrimitive` + `PolylineDash`). Chip PLOCHY in the UKRAJINA panel is only the switch (`setEnabled`); visibility follows the base map (main.js: `ukraineBase.onChange` → show/hide). **Traps measured tonight**: entities were far too heavy — 27 000 polygon entities for 4 tiles blocked the main thread and the globe stayed black for tens of seconds (the first primitive-based build with the stricter draw filter shows 6 889 built-up + 4 824 forest + 1 348 water + 2 702 rail instances for the 4 Lyman tiles, `ready` after ~15 s in workers, frame time ~1 ms); `Cesium.Material.fromType` throws `HTMLCanvasElement is not defined` in Node, so the layer takes a `materialFactory` (tests inject a fake and the layer falls back to per-instance colour / `PolylineColorAppearance` when a material cannot be built); `PolylineColorAppearance` throws „lineWidth is out of range" in Node because `ContextLimits` are 0 without WebGL — the test seeds `_minimumAliasedLineWidth`/`_maximumAliasedLineWidth`; right after switching to KARTA the globe shows only the base colour until the relief tiles arrive from S3 (~0.5 s each, 6 per server in flight, cached on disk afterwards). Verified live at 90 km and 38 km over Lyman: forests dotted, villages grey, reservoirs blue, DeepState and Wikipedia zones and the report markers on top, no console errors. Tests: `scripts/lib/ukraineAreas.test.mjs`, `src/data/ukraineAreas.test.mjs`, `src/data/ukraineAreasLayer.test.mjs`, `ukrainePanel.test.mjs`. The tile download for the remaining directions keeps running in the background (Overpass mirrors alternate between 429 and 504); tiles appear in the app after a reload (meta is read once per show). Not published yet. Next: K3 (soft control zones + 45° hatch, settlement pins by side, city glow), K4, K5.
- **KARTA — after the user's first look: forests off, half-width lines, darker relief, and the real cause of the black globe (render governor tile watchdog)** (2026-09-20, user: „je to nahovno… tie lesy vybodkované sú rušivé a chcel som jemnejšie línie. tie lesy určite nie" and „aj pozadie bolo tmavšie predtým"). (1) **Forests are not drawn**: `AREAS_DRAW_CLASSES = ['built','water','rail']` in `ukraineAreas.js`, `filterAreasForDraw` skips the class (the tile data and the `OkoForestDots` material stay for a possible quiet flat tint later; the dotted texture is gone for good); built-up α 0.36→0.28, water 0.5→0.4, railway 2.2→1.4 px. (2) **Base map style modes**: `ukraineBaseLayer.setStyle('karta'|'default')` with `UKRAINE_BASE_STYLES` (karta: line widths ×0.5, point sizes ×0.72, label fonts ×0.9) — every road/river/oblast polyline and every settlement point/label registers its BASE values at creation (`registerStyle`), so the scale applies to entities that already exist and to village cohorts created later; `removeRecord`/`destroy` drop the registry entries; `main.js` switches the mode from `onActiveMapStackChange` (hillshade stack → karta) and once at boot. (3) **Darker relief**: `KARTA_PALETTE` mid 30/44/58→22/33/45, lit 104/124/146→82/100/120, shadow 7/14/23→5/10/17, amp 2.5→2.0 (the stronger amp lit up every slope). (4) **The black globe was never the imagery**: with the stack restored from the hash and the camera parked, the globe stayed base-colour-black for 121 s — `tilesToRender: 1`, 23 terrain/imagery tiles queued, `RequestScheduler` 0 active requests, last frame two minutes old, governor mode `idle`; 40 manual `scene.render()` calls loaded everything at once. In `requestRenderMode` the globe's load queue is processed only inside a rendered frame, and when the scheduler defers the queued tiles while nothing is in flight there is no completion event to request the next frame, so loading deadlocks until the user moves the mouse. `renderGovernor.js` now arms a **tile watchdog while idle**: every 400 ms (`TILE_WATCH_MS`, `setInterval` unref'd so Node tests do not hang) it requests one frame if `scene.globe.show && !globe.tilesLoaded`; the interval is cleared in continuous mode and re-armed on the last release; `installRenderGovernor(viewer, { timers })` takes injected timers for tests. Verified live: KARTA restored at boot, camera parked, no interaction — `tilesLoaded` true after the wait, globe drawn (the pane's screenshot capture itself times out while the desktop window is hidden, so the proof is the scene state plus the earlier renders). Tests: `renderGovernor.test.mjs` (watchdog arms in idle, silent when loaded or globe hidden, cleared by a hold), `ukraineBaseLayer.test.mjs` (setStyle scales existing and later entities, restores, unknown mode = default), `ukraineAreas.test.mjs` (forest class skipped), `mapStackController.test.mjs` (amp 2.0). Open for the user's next look: line widths/colours of the DeepState outlines and the Wikipedia raster on KARTA (K3), and whether built-up polygons should stay.

The earlier complaint about a too-light background belonged to another map stack, so `KARTA_PALETTE` and `amp: 2.5` are the values of the approved sample again; the halved base-map lines (`ukraineBase.setStyle('karta')`) stay, they were in the proposal from the start. `AREAS_DRAW_CLASSES = ['built','water','rail']` — the dotted forest texture was judged on KARTA itself this time and rejected; tiles and the `OkoForestDots` material remain, re-enabling is adding `'forest'` to that list. Built-up α 0.34, water α 0.5, railway 1.4 px. Tests updated (`ukraineAreas.test.mjs`, `ukraineAreasLayer.test.mjs`, `mapStackController.test.mjs`). Not published yet.
- **KARTA — the green band across central Ukraine was Cesium's fog, now off while KARTA is active** (2026-09-20, user: „je tam taká zelená, to neviem čo je" with a screenshot of a dark-green rhombus from the Belarus border to Mykolaiv). Measured in the pane, layer by layer: not the relief tiles (the same Web Mercator tiles rendered navy next to the band and their textures read back navy through a framebuffer), not DeepState (no polygon spans that area), not the control raster (UA side has alpha 0), not the OSM areas (hidden above 420 km), not DOM overlays (all 37 hidden, band stayed), not globe lighting, ground atmosphere, post-process stages or the sky atmosphere — only `scene.fog.enabled = false` removed it. Cesium's fog takes its colour from the atmosphere model; with the sun below the horizon that colour is greenish and the fog paints it on the terrain with a fairly hard screen-space edge, so on the dark KARTA relief it read as a green band, and because the atmosphere colour follows the sun the band drifted a few degrees between screenshots ten minutes apart (bright basemaps hide the same tint). `MAP_STACKS.karta.fog = false`; `MapStackController._applySceneFog(stack)` runs after every successful switch, remembers the fog state before the first stack that disables it and restores it on any stack without the flag (verified live: fog true → KARTA false → OSM true → KARTA false). Pane traps recorded on the way: `Framebuffer.destroy()` destroys the attached textures by default (pass `destroyAttachments: false` when probing live textures), `scene.render()` without `scene.requestRender()` does not raise `postRender` in requestRenderMode, `gl.readPixels` after a plain render reads a stale buffer. Test added to `mapStackController.test.mjs`.
- **KARTA — stage K3: soft control zones, hatched grey zone, settlement pins by side, city glow** (2026-09-20, user: „pokračuj" after approving K1+K2 on the right globe). All of it is bound to the `karta` style mode, other stacks keep their look. (1) **Control raster** (`ukraineControlLayer.js`): `paintControlCanvas` gained `soft` + `createCanvas` — the RU fills are painted one pixel per cell into a helper canvas and drawn back scaled with image smoothing and a `blur(3px)` filter, the contested hatch stays crisp on top; `CONTROL_STYLES` (karta: soft 3, RU α 0.26, Wikipedia points hidden because the base map pins take over), `setStyle`, `sideAt(lon, lat)` through the pure `nearestSide` (nearest settlement/rural point within 3 km; `mixed` counts as contested). (2) **DeepState** (`ukraineDeepStateLayer.js`): `DEEPSTATE_STYLES` (karta: outlines 1.0/0.7 px instead of 1.8/1.2, grey zone drawn with the new screen-space hatch material), `setStyle`, `buildPolyIndex` + `sideFromPolygons` (occupied/ordlo/crimea/tuzla → ru, grey → contested, otherwise the fallback — the layer passes `ua` whenever the snapshot has occupied polygons, since everything outside them is UA-controlled in DeepState's model) and `sideAt`. (3) **Hatch material** `src/data/screenPatternMaterials.js`: Fabric `OkoHatch45` registered once in Cesium's material cache — 45° lines in SCREEN pixels via `gl_FragCoord` (spacing 9 px, thickness 0.22 of the gap), `HatchMaterialProperty` for entities, `hatchMaterialFor(css, {lineAlpha, fillAlpha})` with a colour fallback when the cache is missing (Node). (4) **Base map** (`ukraineBaseLayer.js`): `setSideResolver(fn)` + `refreshSides()` — in `karta` every settlement point takes `SIDE_PIN_COLORS` (UA #5b8fd0, RU #d0554a, contested #f0a53a) from the resolver, otherwise the class colour; village cohorts created later are coloured at creation; `glowRadiusPx`/`GLOW_MIN_POP` — cities and towns from 10 000 inhabitants get a radial-gradient billboard (`defaultGlowImage`, canvas 64 px, warm #ff5a4a α 0.3, 26–70 px by population, drawn under the pins, shown only in `karta`); `glowImageFactory` injectable for tests. (5) `main.js` composes the resolver (Wikipedia point within 3 km first, then DeepState polygons), refreshes pins on every control/DeepState change and switches control + DeepState styles from `onActiveMapStackChange` like the base map. Measured live over Lyman on KARTA: 1 034 UA / 401 RU / 7 contested pins of 1 442 towns (no grey leftovers once the DeepState fallback is in), 467 town glows, DeepState 229 features with the grey zone hatched, Wikipedia RU raster with soft edges, Wikipedia points hidden. Tests: `screenPatternMaterials.test.mjs`, additions in `ukraineControlLayer.test.mjs` (nearestSide, soft painting on a fake canvas), `ukraineDeepStateLayer.test.mjs` (index, sides, fallback), `ukraineBaseLayer.test.mjs` (pins by side, glow, style switch). Not published yet. Next: K4 (report places as lightning badges, road badges, DeepState attack arrows only with consent), K5 (title, legend, inset, export).

V štýle `karta` sa sídla menované v dennom hlásení Generálneho štábu (vrstva STRETY, `ukraineReportLayer.js`) kreslia ako blesk (kontakt) namiesto bodu; inde ostáva bod. `defaultBoltImage(css)` nakreslí bleskovú cestu (`BOLT_PATH` v štvorci 28) do plátna v DPR, sfarbenú intenzitou útokov (rovnaká škála `reportIntensityColor`: sivá/svetlá/jantár/červená), s tmavou svätožiarou a svetlým rámom; farbu pečie priamo (billboard.color biely), obrázky sa cachujú podľa css. - **KARTA — K4 (časť 1): odznaky ciest (road shields)** (2026-09-20, user: „K4 ale s maximálnym citom pre detail"). Malé štítky s číslom cesty ako na Rybarovej mape, len v štýle `karta`. V `ukraineBaseLayer.js`: kód sa latinizuje (`roadRefDisplay`: М-03 → M-03, cyrilika → latinka), triedu určuje prvé písmeno (`roadRefKind`: M/H = national modrá, E = european zelená, P/T/O/C/A/R = regional oceľová, inak other sivá), `roadShieldSpecs` vráti primárny kód + prípadné druhé európske číslo (dva odznaky vedľa seba ako reálne značenie). Hover odznaku ukáže tú istú kartu ako čiara cesty (`_byEntityId`). Namerané: celý front = 1 583 odznakov, na pohľade smeru Lyman (159 km, −64°) ich je na obrazovke 37 (M-03 sa opakuje v troch bunkách, P-78/79 a T-21-xx okolo). Testy: `roadRefDisplay/roadRefKind/roadShieldSpecs`, geometria, `defaultRoadShieldImage` s falošným plátnom, a build (odznak len s ref, skrytý v default, viditeľný v karta, dohľad = strop). - **UKRAJINA — LIVE obnova zónových snímok (kontrola + DeepState)** (2026-09-20, user: „daj hodinovú ak to má zmysel"). Doteraz `applyControl`/`applyDeepState` v `ukraineTimeline.js` mali strážcu `if (day === _controlDay) return`, takže v LIVE sa snímka toho istého dňa nikdy nenačítala znova — front sa hýbal len pri zmene dňa alebo pri prepnutí čipu. Archivár pritom ťahá DeepState každú hodinu a Wikipédiu každých 6 h. Teraz: v LIVE sa ten istý deň pri každom 60 s tiku (a každom `load()`) pýta znova, ale iba pri inej identite snímky (`sameZoneSnapshot`: kontrola podľa `revisionAt` revízie Wikipédie, DeepState podľa `at` upstream snímky) sa raster/polygóny prestavajú — inak sa nič nekreslí nadarmo. Zlyhaná obnova (sieť) nechá poslednú snímku aj text legendy na mape (nezmaže front pri výpadku). V prehrávaní sa ten istý deň nepýta znova (strážca `state.mode !== 'live'`). Aby obnova naozaj dosiahla server, klientský sklad `createUkraineEventStore` skrátil `controlTtlMs` 60 → 15 min (platí pre kontrolu aj DeepState; payload ~260 kB je za proxy s `max-age=60`, takže ≤ 15 min latencia a lacno). `_controlTask`/`_deepstateTask` bránia súbežnému dvojnásobnému dopytu. Test „LIVE obnova zón…" v `ukraineTimeline.test.mjs` (tik prekreslí len pri novej revízii, chyba nechá mapu, prehrávanie sa nepýta) + export `sameZoneSnapshot`.
- **KARTA — K3 follow-up: city glow fades with camera distance** (2026-09-20, user: „neviem čo sú tie červené fľaky" over a theatre-scale view of eastern Ukraine). The glow billboards are sized in screen pixels (26–70 px), so from ~500 km every town from 10 000 inhabitants painted a fixed-size red disc and hundreds of them merged into blotches over Kharkiv, Dnipro, Poltava or Kryvyi Rih — places where a glow has nothing to say. Fix in `ukraineBaseLayer.js`: `GLOW_FULL_M` 240 km (full strength; a front direction preset sits ~180–230 km from the camera at −64°), `GLOW_GONE_M` 480 km — between them `translucencyByDistance` 1→0 and `scaleByDistance` 1→0.45, `distanceDisplayCondition` ends at 480 km, so the theatre view draws no glow at all. Verified in the pane on KARTA: preset (159 km, −64°) glows on, 340 km nadir faint halos, 600 km nadir none; the uniform red in the east is the soft Wikipedia RU raster, which is correct. Test extended (the K3 test checks the NearFarScalar ranges and the DDC). Front-line dynamics for the record (asked by the user the same day): both zone layers are day snapshots picked by the timeline cursor — Wikipedia control = nearest archived revision on or before the cursor day (241 snapshots since 2022, archiver every 6 h, weekly backfill via CLI), DeepState = nearest archived day on or before (hourly archive since 19 Sep 2026, older days answer 404 → „no snapshot" line; the history endpoint needs DeepState's consent); a redraw is a step per day, no interpolation between snapshots; settlement pins recolour on every snapshot change. Known gap: in LIVE mode a newer snapshot of the same day is not re-fetched until the day changes or the chip is toggled.
- **UKRAINE — how old the source is, said out loud** (2026-09-21, user picked it from a menu after the restart: „hlasnejšie priznať starnutie zdroja"). The control legend printed the Wikipedia revision date and nothing else, so a front line from 13 August 2026 looked exactly as trustworthy as yesterday's — nobody subtracts dates in their head. Measured on the live archive: our daily snapshot of that day carries `revisionAt: 2026-08-13T09:28:11Z`, because nobody has edited `Module:Russo-Ukrainian war detailed map` since (the overview module has stood still since 3 May), i.e. the map was 39 days stale with no warning. New pure module `src/data/ukraineFreshness.js`: `ageDays` (whole days down, ISO string or epoch ms, a future date yields 0 rather than −1 so a skewed clock cannot print „−1 days ago"), `freshnessOf` → `{ ageDays, stale }` in the same shape `gasPrices.js`/`gasFlows.js` already use, `ageTextKey`/`ageText` (Slovak needs three forms — „dnes", „pred 1 dňom", „pred 39 dňami" — so a plain `{n} days` would produce „pred 1 dňami"). Thresholds belong to the source, not to the app: `CONTROL_STALE_DAYS` 14 (a volunteer map updated irregularly; shorter would fire on an ordinary pause, longer would have missed exactly this five-week gap), `DEEPSTATE_STALE_DAYS` 4 (we show DeepState with a deliberate 2–3 day delay, so day four means the source or our archiver has stopped). `ukraineTimeline.js` renders it into its own element next to „as of" (`.oko-ukr-tl-ctl-age`, exposed through `_getStateForTest`) rather than appending to the translated string: above the threshold it gains the word STALE, an amber bordered badge and a title explaining what it means. Verified live at `?front=lyman`: control „stav k 13.8.2026 · revízia mapy Wikipédie" + „pred 39 dňami · ZASTARANÉ" (amber), DeepState „stav k 20.9.2026 06:09 UTC · …" + „pred 1 dňom" (muted, no badge). Tests: `ukraineFreshness.test.mjs` (8, including the real 13 August case and the off-by-one at exactly the threshold) and a new timeline test covering fresh → stale → fresh again and layer-off clearing the badge. i18n `ukraine.age.*`, `ukraine.src.stale*` (SK+EN).
- **Night lights as a daily GIBS overlay — and the first layer token outside a–z0–9** (2026-09-21, the second item the user picked after the restart). New data layer `gibs-night-lights`, one more entry in the existing `GIBS_OVERLAYS` catalogue, so no new plumbing: `VIIRS_SNPP_DayNightBand_At_Sensor_Radiance`, Level 8, daily PNG. The product choice was measured, not assumed — `VIIRS_SNPP_DayNightBand_ENCC` is dead (last day 2023-07-07), `VIIRS_NOAA20_DayNightBand_At_Sensor_Radiance` only starts 2024-03-25 (no pre-invasion baseline), `VIIRS_NOAA20_DayNightBand` is Level 7 and answers HTTP 400 at Level 8; the SNPP at-sensor series runs 2020-11-18 → today and returned tiles for 2021-01-15, 2022-02-23 (the day before the invasion) and 2022-10-11 (the first large strikes on the grid), which is what makes it worth anything to the UKRAINE timeline. **Honesty**: this is radiance at the sensor, not the gap-filled BRDF-corrected Black Marble product (VNP46A2 has no GIBS colormap and would therefore carry no legend), so moonlight, cloud tops and aurora raise the signal — a dark city is evidence, not proof, of a blackout; it is also a different layer from the static 2016 Black Marble composite in `nightLights.js`, which can never show tonight. Legend is five real anchors read out of the v1.3 colormap XML (0 → 38+ nW/cm²sr). **Token alphabet extended**: `LAYER_STATE_REGISTRY` had used every one of `a–z0–9` (36 layers, 36 tokens), so a 37th layer could not be added at all; the validator now accepts `[a-zA-Z0-9]` and night lights takes `N`. Decoding stays case-sensitive, so existing share links are unaffected — but `decodeLayerStateParams` deliberately rejects a whole payload containing an unknown token, which means a link carrying `N` will not work on a build published before this change. Verified live over Ukraine at 1 200 km on the OSM stack: 28 tiles fetched, layer alpha 0.85, Kyiv/Kharkiv/Dnipro legible under the control points. The shared GIBS zoom fade applies (Level 8 → invisible below 200 km, full above 600 km), so the layer is meant for the many-cities-at-once view rather than street scale. Tests: catalogue and level pins updated in `gibsOverlays.test.mjs`, registry count 36 → 37 in `layerState.test.mjs`. DATA_SOURCES row for the GIBS science overlays extended with the product, its date range and the at-sensor caveat.
- **Ukraine front directions by voice — `show_front`** (2026-09-21, the third item the user picked after the restart; the aliases had been deferred since the UKRAINE plan). New pure resolver `resolveFrontScene(query, translate)` in `ukraineFrontScenes.js` matches a spoken direction in the four forms a person actually uses: the id (`lyman`), the Ukrainian General Staff name from the daily report (`Лиманський`, apostrophes normalised), the English name (`Lyman direction`) and the translated name (`Lymanský smer`) — folding diacritics so `lymansky smer` lands too, and reaching city names carried in the translated parentheses (`Myrnohrad` → pokrovsk). Latin folding only: NFD would turn Ukrainian `й` into `и`, so Cyrillic is matched in its original form through the existing `frontSceneByGsDirection`. **An ambiguous query resolves to `null` and frames nothing** — flying to the wrong sector of a front is worse than asking, so the action returns the list instead. New voice action `show_front` (`gevActions.js`) with actions `list`/`show`, deliberately separate from `control_scene`: that one drives cinematic playback that runs for minutes and can be stopped, this is one camera jump that returns immediately. The runner takes `getFrontScenes` as a lazy reader because the presets are wired late in `main.js` boot (after the UKRAINE panel) while voice initialises earlier. Tool schema added to `GEV_REALTIME_TOOLS` in `vite.config.js` plus one instruction line telling the model to use `show_front`, not `control_scene`, for a named sector. **Two frozen baselines were re-derived openly, not loosened**: the byte-length + SHA-256 pin in `firstRunExperience.test.mjs` (its subject — first-run missions riding existing tools — is untouched; the guard fires on any schema edit, and a schema edit busts the voice session cache) and the unchanged-tools digest in `radioMarkup.test.mjs`, whose own comment prescribes re-deriving on a deliberate change; inventory 28 → 29 tools, 19 → 20 unchanged. **NOT verified end to end**: `OPENAI_API_KEY` is empty, so no realtime session can run and nobody has heard the model call this tool. What is verified is everything below that line — the resolver (11 tests incl. ambiguity and diacritics), the action (list, framing, ambiguous query framing nothing, missing presets during early boot) and the static fact that `show_front` reaches the session config with a well-formed schema.
- **DeepState stays local until consent arrives — a host gate, not a kill switch** (2026-09-21, before publishing the UKRAINE module). Publishing the module would have made `/api/ukraine/events/deepstate` reachable from the public domain, and DeepState's licence §2 prohibits "distribution, publication, proxying" without authorisation; the request was submitted on 2026-09-19 and the answer is still outstanding. The existing `UKRAINE_DEEPSTATE=off` was not the right instrument — it stops the daily archiver, so it would have punched a hole in the archive on the very day the answer is due. Instead the read route decides per REQUEST: new pure `deepstateAllowedForHost(host, {consent})` in `ukraineEventsProxy.js` reads the `Host` header, not the address — the Cloudflare Tunnel connects to localhost:4173, so `remoteAddress` is 127.0.0.1 for a visitor from the internet exactly as it is for the owner and would distinguish nothing (same reasoning as `basemapForHost()` for Stadia). Localhost, 127.0.0.1, `[::1]`, bare `::1` and `*.localhost` pass; anything else, including a missing header, gets **HTTP 451** `deepstate_consent_pending` — fail closed. IPv6 needs care: stripping `:port` blindly turns bare `::1` into `:`, so the port comes off only when the host holds a single colon. `UKRAINE_DEEPSTATE=consent` opens it everywhere the day DeepState answers; `=off` still disables the archiver as before. The client says so honestly instead of showing a network error — `ukraineTimeline.js` maps 451 to `ukraine.ds.disabled`, whose text was rewritten from the old env-var wording to "not served publicly until DeepState answers the consent request" (the key existed but had never been used). Verified through the running dev server: `Host: localhost` → HTTP 200 with the 2026-09-20 snapshot, `Host: oko.uhrin.digital` → HTTP 451. Tests in `ukraineEventsProxy.test.mjs` cover both host classes, the tunnel reasoning and the consent override. Everything else in the UKRAINE module publishes normally; if DeepState declines, the layer, the job and the archive still get deleted as planned.
- **A source that misdates itself, and a layer that oversold itself** (2026-09-22, user picked both items). **(1) The General Staff report can carry the wrong date.** The 11 September 2026 ArmyInform article literally reads „станом на 8:00 11 **серпня**" — 11 August. The parser read it correctly; the system trusted it, which cost two days of archive: the September report was filed under 2026-08-11 and overwrote the genuine August one (212 clashes replaced by 235) while 2026-09-11 stayed empty. `parseGeneralStaffReport` now compares the „станом на" stamp against the article's publication time and, past `REPORT_STAMP_DRIFT_MS` (36 h), files and labels by publication — the summary is published ~05:11 UTC on the morning it describes, so the normal gap is minutes and publication is the sounder anchor. When it drifts the card shows the date only (`11.9.`), never an invented hour, and keeps `stampDrifted` + `sourceStampText` so the panel can explain in a tooltip what the source actually claimed instead of looking like OKO made the date up. `scripts/lib/ukraineArchive.mjs` imports the same constant rather than keeping a second copy of 36 h — two copies would drift apart and the archive would file somewhere other than the card shows. Archive repaired and backfilled: 2026-07-25 → 2026-09-22, **56 of 60 days**; the four missing (9, 20, 22 Aug, 9 Sep) were checked against ArmyInform's own daily archive pages and carry no General Staff summary at all — holes in the source, not in us. **(2) CONTROL renamed to SETTLEMENT CONTROL** (`ukraine.part.control`, both the panel and timeline chips). The layer is per-settlement status parsed from a Wikipedia module whose 2026 edit history is three edits (3 May, 10 Jul, 13 Aug) — measured this day — so calling it „control" invited reading it as a surveyed front line. The zone fills are equally derived, so both legends now say so: `ukraine.ctl.ru` → „Russian control (derived)" and `ukraine.karta.legend.ru` → „Russian control (derived from settlements)", matching the „combat zone (derived)" that was already honest. Verified live at `?front=lyman`: both chips read SETTLEMENT CONTROL, the legend reads „ruská kontrola (odvodené)", and a report record for 11 Sep renders „stav k 11.9." with the tooltip naming „08:00 11.8." as the source's own claim. Tests: 4 new parser cases (drift, threshold exactly at the boundary, no publication time to compare against) and 2 archive cases.
- **The photo strip was mostly not photos** (2026-09-22, user circled the media strip: „oprav chýbajúce foto video"). Two thirds of the tiles under FOTKY A VIDEÁ were blank squares. Measured against the live payload for 22 Sep: of 292 media items in the window, **196 were `telegram/text` with no attachment at all** — mostly „Повітряні сили ЗСУ" drone alerts and DeepState map-update notes that carry only words. The parser was never wrong: `parseTelegramPreview` already tags those `kind: 'text'` with `thumb: null`, and a spot check confirmed it (DeepStateUA/23849 genuinely has no image while 23850 and 23851 do). What was wrong is that `mediaInWindow` passed everything through, so a strip named photos-and-videos filled with text posts and counted them. New pure `hasVisualMedia(m)` in `ukraineEventsClient.js` keeps an item when it is a video (`kind` or `videos > 0`) or carries `thumb`/`photos`; `mediaInWindow` filters on it, which fixes the strip and its counter together since both read the same function. **A video without a poster stays**: ArmyInform RSS enclosures (`provider: 'file'`) are playable mp4s and the feed was checked — it carries the enclosure and no `media:thumbnail`, no `img` in the content — so the ▶ glyph on them is a correct rendering, not a missing image. Live result at `?front=lyman`: empty tiles **25 → 6** (all six genuine posterless videos), counter **327 → 96 fotiek/videí**. Tests: 7 cases on `hasVisualMedia` incl. a photo without a thumbnail and a posterless video, plus one on `mediaInWindow` proving text drops out while event context survives. Possible follow-up, not done: fetch an og:image for those posterless videos through the existing `/api/link-image`.
- **UKRAINE UI tidy-up A1–A4** (2026-09-23, user picked group A from a list of UI proposals: „začni s A ale deep state neruš skry"). Commits `20da229` · `39afdd7` · `2a8071f` · `78f9502`. **A1 — DeepState hidden where the server refuses it, nothing removed.** The timeline reacts to the server's answer rather than keeping a second copy of the host rule in the browser: the first HTTP 451 calls `blockDeepState()` (hide the legend row, hide the layer, clear the snapshot, stop asking — the front scene calls `showDeepState` on every direction switch), `isDeepStateAvailable()` lets the panel hide its chip. Archive, code and local display stay; when consent arrives (`UKRAINE_DEEPSTATE=consent`) the server answers 200 and it all comes back. A 502 is still reported, not hidden. Trap: `.oko-ukr-tl-ctl` has `display:flex`, which beats the `hidden` attribute — the fake DOM hid the row, a real browser would not; `.oko-ukr-tl-ctl[hidden]{display:none}` added. **A2 — staleness visible on the map, and age measured against the VIEWED day.** A stale Wikipedia snapshot now paints the RU fill and the combat-zone hatch at half strength (`STALE_DIM` 0.5 in `ukraineFreshness.js`, pure `controlZoneAlphas`), and the legend swatches in the timeline and on the exported KARTA dim by the same value so map and legend never disagree. Doing it exposed a second bug: age was computed against „now", so replaying 1 Mar 2022 would have shown the 24 Feb 2022 revision as „1 670 days · STALE" with a dimmed map. New `viewedRefMs(requestedAt, now)` returns the end of the viewed day (never the future); in LIVE it is exactly „now". Measured on real zone pixels: LIVE 23 Sep → 41 days, stale, RU fill alpha 38/255; replay 1 Mar 2022 → 5 days, fresh, alpha 77/255. **A4 — one „sources" line.** Pure `freshnessRow()`: Wikipedia, DeepState (only when available), GS report, events — each with its own threshold (14 / 4 / 2 / 2 days: `REPORT_STALE_DAYS`, `EVENTS_STALE_DAYS`), same viewed-day reference as A2; a source without a date is left out. Amber dot and text when stale, tooltip with the full date and age. One `reportForView()` now feeds both the counter and this line. **A3 — collapsible timeline**, collapsed by default and remembered per viewer (`oko.ukraine.timeline.collapsed`, all storage access in try/catch). Collapsed keeps the controls, histogram, sources line, media strip and the layer CHIPS (toggles, not legend) side by side; swatches, „as of" lines, ages, counts and the event-type legend show on expand. An active type filter keeps its legend visible (`has-filter`). Measured at 1007×960: 218 px collapsed vs 371 px expanded. **Found along the way:** the left rail never reacted to the timeline's height. `_initLeftPanelAdaptiveLayout` collects obstacles once and only visible ones (`:not([hidden])`); the Ukraine timeline is created later and starts hidden, so its ResizeObserver was never attached. New `StyleManager.observeLeftStackObstacle(element)`, called from `main.js` right after the timeline is created; a spy confirmed 5 relayout requests on expand where there had been none. Honest limit: expanded on a 960 px window still overlaps the rail by ~107 px — capacity, not trigger (rail starts at y=267, collapsed headers need 354 px, expanded timeline 371 px). Before A3 that was the permanent state; collapsed (default) leaves 46 px to spare. Suite 3768/3768.
- **UKRAINE direction card (B5) + General Staff report parser fixes** (2026-09-23, user: „pokračuj karta smeru"). Picking a front direction in the UKRAJINA panel now opens a card right under that direction's button (the list has 12 rows; a card below it would be off-screen): attacks in the shown report, 30 daily columns from the report archive, the average of the last 7 days against the rest of the window with a RISING / FALLING / STEADY word (only with ≥ 4 recent and ≥ 7 earlier days; a difference under 1.5 attacks/day or 20 % is „steady", so 1 → 2 attacks is not „+100 %"), and the settlements the reports named on most of the last 14 days (click = camera over the place from the south, `flyToBoundingSphere`, 22 km, −58°). The whole front shows total combat clashes and no places. The card follows the day of the report on screen (LIVE, or the timeline cursor through the report override). **Honesty rules in the data, not only the text:** a day with no archived report is an empty column (`missing`), a report that does not mention the direction is a dot (`unmentioned`, never 0 — North-Slobozhanskyi is absent on 52 of 57 archived days), a count the text does not state is a grey tick, and an afternoon interim report („станом на 16:00", seven archived days between 25 Jul and 3 Aug) is a hatched column kept out of every average (`isPartialReport`). The note says the counts are one side's claims (UA General Staff via ArmyInform, CC BY 4.0), not verified. Pieces: `/api/ukraine/events/directions?from&to` (≤ 92 days, only direction paragraphs + total + report time, 10 min cache; `directionsPayload` in `scripts/lib/ukraineArchive.mjs`), `store.directions()` in `ukraineEventsClient.js` (shared in-flight request, failures not cached), pure `src/data/ukraineDirectionTrend.js` (reuses `reportByScene` and `directionPlaces`, the same functions as the map markers), DOM in `src/ukraineDirectionCard.js`, wired through `createUkrainePanel({ directionCard })` in `main.js`. **The card exposed parser bugs, fixed in `ukraineReport.js`:** (1) the total only knew „бойових зіткнень"/„бойові зіткнення" — „221 бойове зіткнення" (numbers ending in 1) and „261 боєзіткнення" left 11 of 57 days without a total (the fix recovered 4; the rest are interim reports that carry none), and on 30 Aug the unrecognised summary sentence gave BOTH Kostiantynivka and Pokrovsk „221 attacks" (`REPORT_TOTAL_RE`); (2) the summary sentence is now cut out of its paragraph instead of skipping the whole paragraph, so „… 231 бойове зіткнення. Найбільше атак … на Костянтинівському напрямку — 35." keeps its 35; (3) a direction first seen in a shared or count-less sentence is replaced by a later paragraph of its own that has a count (30 Aug: 31 and 30 instead of null); (4) „— 31 —" between dashes and „двічі проводив наступальні дії"; (5) „не проводив" (singular) is an explicit 0 — before, one or more directions were „unknown" on 40 of 57 days. **Archive repaired:** `node scripts/build-ukraine-events.mjs --reparse-reports [--from D] [--to D] [--dry-run]` re-downloads each archived article from its stored `url`, re-parses it and writes only days whose total or directions changed (fingerprint ignores order and texts), copying the original to `.gev-cache/ukraine/events/reports-backup/<time>/` first; `fetchedAt` is kept and `reparsedAt` added; a day never moves (different day after parsing = skipped, not overwritten). Run 2026-09-23: 57 checked, 47 rewritten, backup `reports-backup/202609231938`. Still open, honestly: the seven 16:00 interim days stay interim (the morning reports of those days were never archived) and, with 2 Aug, have no total (31 Jul says 62 — an afternoon count). **Capacity limit, not the card:** at 1007×960 with every left-rail panel present the UKRAJINA panel body gets 0 px (rail items end at y≈695, the collapsed timeline starts at y≈754), so the card is only reachable in a taller window; at 1500×1400 the body gets 166 px and the 273 px card scrolls. Verified in the pane on Pokrovsk: 23 attacks for 23 Sep, avg 25.7 vs 33.7 → FALLING, places Serhiivka 14 d, Bilytske 11 d, Dobropillia 11 d …; clicking Serhiivka flew the camera to 18.7 km south of it. New tests: `ukraineReportForms`, `ukraineDirectionTrend`, `ukraineArchiveDirections`, `ukraineEventsProxyDirections`, `ukraineDirectionCard` (existing tests unchanged). Suite 3801/3801.
- **DeepState analytics demo + `/api/deepstate/analytics` — finished after another agent** (2026-09-23, user: „dokonči prácu čo začal ten druhý agent"). The owner gave Antigravity a spec (CesiumJS globe, `Cesium.Ion.defaultAccessToken = 'YOUR_ION_TOKEN_HERE'` left as a placeholder, „yesterday's" `deepstatemap_data_YYYYMMDD.geojson` from the GitHub mirror cyterat/deepstate-map-data, `GeoJsonDataSource.load` with `clampToGround`, red fill 0.4 + solid red outline, `turf.area()` in km², a dashboard „Date / Occupied Area", fallback to older days) and then a server API; that agent staged the files and crashed before committing or documenting. Its prepared commit message called the mirror „ODbL, public, no consent issue" — **false**: GPL-3.0 covers the mirror's scripts only, the data stay under the DeepStateMap.live licence (§2: no distribution/publication/proxying without consent), and the mirror declares no consent. The owner decided to keep it („Nechať — zadal som to ja"), so it is recorded as an explicit, scoped exception: DATA_SOURCES.md row, rule 5 of `docs/drafts/ukrajina-plan.md`; the UKRAJINA layers still use no mirror, and the route is deliberately not host-gated. A read-only review (3 lenses, each finding re-checked by a skeptic) found the work unfinished; fixed: **server** (`src/data/deepstateAnalyticsProxy.js`) — all dates in UTC (the mirror names files by the UTC day of its ~03:00 UTC run; the old mix of a UTC base and local `setDate` was a day off west of UTC and around DST), „yesterday" = UTC calendar day − 1, strict `?date=` (20261399 used to roll over to 2027-04-09) → 400 `bad_date` with reason invalid/future/before_mirror (first mirror file 2024-07-08), `maxFallback=abc` used to give NaN → 404, now the default; 404s are cached (30 min for today/yesterday, 6 h older — before, every request re-walked up to 8 GitHub files); after the first non-404 upstream error (429/5xx/timeout/network) no more GitHub calls — in that request and for ≥ 60 s after it (Retry-After honoured, max 15 min) — and **502 `upstream_unavailable`** instead of a false 404; the whole search has a 30 s budget (503 `upstream_slow`) and a shared single-flight fetch keeps its own 25 s timeout instead of the first caller's budget; an upstream file without a polygon of positive area is neither served nor written to disk (an empty collection used to be pinned forever as „0 km²"); when the mirror stalls longer than the window a default request gets the newest file on disk at most 30 days old with `stale`/`outsideWindow` (an explicit `?date=` gets an honest 404), and a 200 says `upstreamUnavailable` when the older file is shown because the mirror failed, not because newer files do not exist; **found in the second review:** `new URL()` on a path like `/api/deepstate/analytics//x:99999` threw inside the async handler and the unhandled rejection killed the whole dev server (which also serves `/api` for the public site) — now 400 `bad_url` plus a middleware wrapper that turns any handler error into a 500 (verified live: the server stays up); single-flight registered before it can settle; `send()` like ukraineEventsProxy (gzip — 65 725 B → 25 297 B measured, Content-Length, no-store on errors, Retry-After on 429, HEAD without a body); memory (16), negative cache (128) and limiter (500 keys) bounded; every 200 carries `attribution`, `license`, `note`, `areaMethod`, `requestedDate`, `stale`. **Page** (`public/demos/deepstate-analytics/`) — the placeholder line stays literally as specified, but with it ion answers 401 and the globe was blank while the badge said „Ready": now the page detects the placeholder, uses Natural Earth II from `/cesium` on the plain ellipsoid and says so; without WebGL the figures are still computed (badge „No globe"); the camera frames Ukraine as a rectangle around the specified centre (≈ 31.17 E, 48.38 N) so Crimea fits any aspect ratio (phones: extended south, the card covers the lower half); Cesium drops outlines on clamped polygons (GeometryUpdater), so the requested solid red outline is drawn as clamped polylines along every ring and hole (22 rings on the 2026-09-22 file); permanent source line (according to DeepStateMap.live, via the unofficial mirror, not a live front line, not for evacuation or route planning, not affiliated), sub-lines (mirror file date UTC, DeepState lags 2–3 days; area incl. Crimea and areas occupied since 2014, OKO estimate — `turf.area` and the server agree at 116 783 km²); amber „Older file" badge when a fallback day is shown; the direct mirror download runs only when the OKO API is absent (static hosting) — an API 404/429/502 is shown, not re-downloaded 7× from GitHub; Turf 7.4.0 pinned with SRI; the dead CDN fallback for Cesium removed (it pointed at 1.124 and could never fire); monochrome map icon instead of the 🇺🇦 emoji (Windows shows „UA"); title „OKO · Occupied area per DeepState", `noindex`; card with a 16 px gutter, height capped (the middle scrolls, the source line never does), at the bottom on phones above the Cesium credits (checked at 320×568 and 375×812: no horizontal scroll, date and area visible, 48 % of the screen); the direct mirror path skips broken/empty files and has a 25 s timeout; 429 retries after Retry-After (10–300 s). Page URL is `/demos/deepstate-analytics/index.html` (the bare directory falls back to the app on the dev server). The DeepState host gate of 2026-09-21 covers `/api/ukraine/events/deepstate` only; this route and its disk cache `.gev-cache/deepstate-analytics/` are outside the „delete if DeepState declines" plan by the owner's keep decision. Tests: the other agent's 8 tests unchanged and passing, new `deepstateAnalyticsProxyHardening.test.mjs` (UTC in New York/Kiritimati/Bratislava via in-process `process.env.TZ` — the shell TZ is ignored on Windows, 400s, negative cache, 502, disk fallback, outsideWindow, empty file not stored, single-flight + retry after a sync throw, cooldown after a 429 with Retry-After, bad URL, bounded stale disk, 503 on budget, honesty fields, gzip/HEAD/405/429, 600-IP limiter; temp dirs removed). A second read-only review (3 lenses + skeptics) re-checked the fixes; every confirmed item is fixed above. The demo ships to the public site on the next publish. Suite 3814/3814 under Node 24 (the 3 known worldOverlayAllocation failures unchanged). **Update 2026-09-24:** the demo page was deleted by the owner („omyl v prompte"); the endpoint and the mirror core stay.
- **DeepState layer from the cyterat mirror on public hosts; plan rule „no mirrors" deleted** (2026-09-24, owner: „Sprav 3 a vymaž toto pravidlo" — option 3 of: link the demo / area figure in the panel / mirror as the layer's source on the domain). Before, `/api/ukraine/events/deepstate` answered **451** on every non-local host (our own archive of the DeepState API stays local until DeepState consents, licence §2), so the public site had no DeepState polygons at all. Now: **public host → a snapshot built from the mirror** (`deepstateSnapshotFromMirror` in `ukraineDeepState.js`, `source: 'mirror'`, day of the file, `at` = file day 03:00 UTC with `atApprox`, only `occupied` polygons — the mirror merges occupied + Crimea + ORDLO into one MultiPolygon and has no grey zone, liberated areas, attack directions or airfields); **localhost → our archive first** (`source: 'archive'`, 227 features on 23 Sep incl. grey zone), and the mirror only for days the archive does not cover (it starts 2026-09-19; the mirror has files since 2024-07-08, so replay history now reaches mid-2024). Days before the mirror → honest 404 `source: mirror, firstDay`; mirror down → 502 `deepstate_mirror_unavailable` (not „no data"); future days clamp to today; a fallback file caused by a mirror outage is not cached. `UKRAINE_DEEPSTATE_MIRROR=off` restores the old 451, `UKRAINE_DEEPSTATE=off` still switches everything off. The mirror logic (memory/disk/404 cache, single-flight, backoff after errors, 30 s budget, file validation) moved into an exported core `createDeepStateMirror()` in `deepstateAnalyticsProxy.js`; `vite.config.js` creates ONE instance (`sharedDeepStateMirror()`) for both routes, so the demo and the layer share caches and the GitHub backoff. **UI honesty:** layer state carries `source`; the timeline row says „súbor z <date> · DeepStateMap.live cez neoficiálny mirror cyterat/deepstate-map-data · samotný DeepState mešká 2–3 dni" (date only — DeepState's own time is unknown from the mirror), the area line „okupované vrátane Krymu a území od 2014 ≈ N km² (… mirror šedú zónu nemá)", swatches for grey/liberated/attack/airfield are hidden (`.is-mirror`), a mirror 404 says the mirror's history starts 8 Jul 2024, a mirror outage says so; KARTA names „DeepState (cez mirror)" and drops the grey-zone legend item. Measured: public host 24 Sep → file of 23 Sep (today's not yet there), 21 polygons, 116 783 km²; 1 Jun 2026 → 18 polygons, 116 586 km²; 1 Jan 2024 → 404; localhost replay 10 Sep 2026 → mirror (21 polygons, 116 789 km²) drawn on the map, LIVE → archive. **Docs:** rule 5 („no mirrors") removed from `docs/drafts/ukrajina-plan.md` entirely on the owner's follow-up („vymaž … žiadne mirrory. Budeme ich používať"; later rules renumbered 5–7, the stage line and the „checked and unused" list no longer call mirrors forbidden; the EU-sanctions „mirror clause" in rule 1 is about sanctioned media domains and stays), the source row split (cyterat ✅ owner decision with the licence facts; longlinecode stays ❌ as an ISW derivative); DATA_SOURCES.md: the mirror row now covers the layer and the demo, the API row says the archive stays local while public hosts get the mirror. The licence facts are unchanged by the decision (GPL-3.0 = scripts only, data under the DeepState licence, no consent) and stay written everywhere the data is shown. Tests: new `src/data/ukraineDeepStateMirror.test.mjs`; existing tests unchanged. **A read-only review (2 lenses + skeptics) confirmed 9 issues, all fixed:** the collapsed timeline (default) only showed „DeepState" in the SOURCES row — now „DeepState (cez mirror)" with a tooltip naming the mirror; the chip tooltip still said „consent requested" — now `ukraine.ds.note-mirror` states the licence facts and that DeepState gave no consent (timeline and panel); the Wikipedia combat band was hidden whenever DeepState drew, so with the grey-zone-less mirror the public map lost its contested zone — now the band stays and only the Wikipedia RU fill is hidden (`control.setRuFillVisible`), KARTA legend adds „zóna bojov (Wikipédia)" and credits Wikipedia too; KARTA pins in the grey zone turned UA-blue because `sideAt` fell back to „ua" outside the occupied polygon — with the mirror it returns null there; KARTA credited DeepState while it drew nothing (loading/404/502) — only when it has features; „no snapshot (archived since 19 Sep)" showed while the mirror request was still running — now „loading"; LIVE refresh compared only `at` (fixed 03:00 for a mirror file) — now also source and the outage flag; a mirror 404 inside its history no longer claims „history from 8 Jul 2024"; a snapshot aliased under its own day now carries that day as `requestedAt` (age was measured from the wrong day). Found on the server: the per-IP limiter keyed on the FIRST X-Forwarded-For value, which the client controls (Cloudflare appends the real IP after it) — `clientKey` now uses CF-Connecting-IP, then the socket, for all `/api/ukraine/events` routes. The layer registers a Cesium credit „DeepStateMap.live (via mirror …)" while it draws, and `buildAttributionLine` puts DeepState right after Google/Cesium ion so the 6-part share credit never drops it. Rejected by the skeptics: archive-first on localhost, `UKRAINE_DEEPSTATE=off` semantics, 404 wording after a >7-day mirror stall, limiter tuning. Suite 3822/3822 under Node 24 (the 3 known worldOverlayAllocation failures unchanged). **Update 2026-09-24:** the demo page was deleted by the owner („omyl v prompte"); the endpoint and the mirror core stay.
- **Backup DeepState mirror lazar-bit; the standalone demo deleted** (2026-09-24). The owner pasted a comparison of the two public DeepState repos („použi toto ak to už nie je") and, mid-way, asked to delete the demo („demo vymaž, bol to omyl v prompte"). **Demo:** `public/demos/deepstate-analytics/` removed (its URL now falls back to the app); `/api/deepstate/analytics` stays (no page uses it any more — the owner was asked whether to drop it too). **cyterat** was already the source. **lazar-bit/deepstate-map-data-analytics** is a fork whose own GitHub Actions scrape DeepState daily at 03:00 UTC (`script.py`, `update.yml`), so it is a real backup: on 2026-09-24 it had 804 daily files vs cyterat's 802, the extra two being 2025-11-26 and 2026-07-09. `DEEPSTATE_MIRRORS` lists both; `createDeepStateMirror({ mirrors })` tries them in order — a 404, an unusable file or an error at one tries the next; only when none has the file and none failed is the day negatively cached; when none has it and one failed, the first error drives the backoff as before. The default is cyterat only (the other agent's tests count GitHub calls and stay unchanged); `vite.config.js` gives the shared production core both. Provenance travels with the day: `found.mirror`, a `<day>.mirror` sidecar on disk for non-cyterat files (so it survives a restart), `snapshot.mirror`, the layer's `getState().mirror`, and every label names the repo the day really came from (timeline „súbor z … cez neoficiálny mirror {repo}", SOURCES tooltip, Cesium credit, API `attribution` via `deepstateMirrorAttribution`). Measured on the public-host route: 2025-11-26 → lazar-bit (10 polygons, 115 366 km²) where cyterat alone would have shown 25 Nov; 2026-07-09 → lazar-bit; other days → cyterat. **Not used:** the fork's `aggregated_deepstatemap.csv` (51 MB, one row per polygon per day with centroid and WKT) — its `area` column is Shapely `polygon.area` on EPSG:4326, i.e. square DEGREES (first row: 12.9 for a polygon of roughly 110 000 km²), so the pasted claim that it gives km² growth directly is wrong; km² would have to be recomputed from the geometry, which OKO already does per day (`polygonAreaKm2`). **Review (1 reviewer + skeptic) fixed:** the analytics answer named cyterat in `attribution` and `license.mirrorUrl` even for a fork day — now `deepstateAnalyticsAttribution` / `deepstateMirrorLicense` per mirror in both routes; a mirror's error was dropped when the other answered, so cyterat was asked again 1 s after „Retry-After: 600" and a hanging cyterat ate 25 s of the 30 s budget on every new day — now each mirror has its own pause (`mirrorBackoff`, ≥ 60 s / Retry-After, max 15 min), a paused mirror is skipped, „not there" is only claimed when no mirror was paused, and one mirror gets 12 s so both fit the budget; the `<day>.mirror` sidecar is written atomically BEFORE the day file, removed if that write fails and when cyterat later serves the day, and an unknown sidecar value or a non-ENOENT read error is not trusted/cached. Suite 3825/3825 under Node 24 (the 3 known worldOverlayAllocation failures unchanged).
- **Thinner Wikipedia combat band** (2026-09-24, owner after seeing the public-domain view: „stenši pás"). With the mirror (no grey zone) the derived Wikipedia band stays on the map and at front-scene zoom it read as broad amber bars. Two changes in `src/ukraineControlLayer.js`: (1) **narrower band** — `controlRaster` got an optional `contestedKm` (default = `bandKm`, so old callers and tests are unchanged) and the layer now passes `CONTROL_BAND_KM = 5` (sides equidistant ±5 km, was 7) and `CONTROL_CONTESTED_KM = 4` (radius around contested settlements, was 7). Measured on the 24 Sep Wikipedia snapshot: 578 band cells instead of 845, 0 direct RU|UA edges (a gap in the band); 4.5/3.5 km already leaves 3 gaps, so 5/4 is the narrowest continuous setting on 0.05° cells. (2) **thin stripes** — `CONTROL_RASTER_SCALE` 4 → 8 px per cell (canvas 2 976 × 1 344), line width 0.14 cell (≈ 0.5 km, was 0.35) and spacing 2 cells (was 2.5); the KARTA blur is scaled by `scale / 4` so its soft edges look the same. Verified in the pane on the Pokrovsk scene with a mirror day (15 Sep). New test `src/data/ukraineControlBand.test.mjs`; existing tests unchanged. Suite 3827/3827 under Node 24 (the 3 known worldOverlayAllocation failures unchanged).
- **`/api/deepstate/analytics` deleted; mirror core moved to `src/data/deepstateMirror.js`** (2026-09-24, owner: „zmaž ak je to potrebné" — it was: nothing used the endpoint after the demo page was deleted, yet it was public, served the same DeepState data a second way and had already once been able to crash the dev server). `deepstateAnalyticsProxy.js` is gone; the core (`createDeepStateMirror`, mirrors cyterat + lazar-bit, per-mirror pause, provenance, file validation) lives on unchanged in `deepstateMirror.js` — function bodies byte-identical per the review, only the User-Agent suffix changed; renamed `DEEPSTATE_CYTERAT_BASE`, `DEEPSTATE_MIRROR_LICENSE`, `DEEPSTATE_MIRROR_NOTE`; removed endpoint-only pieces (HTTP handler, gzip send, limiter, `getClientIp`, `parseMaxFallback`, `formatDisplayDate`, `deepstateAnalyticsAttribution`, area-method text) and the „newest file on disk outside the window" branch, which only the endpoint's default request could reach (the layer always asks for a specific day). The cache directory keeps its name `.gev-cache/deepstate-analytics/`. `vite.config.js` no longer registers the endpoint and creates the core inline for `ukraineEventsProxy`. The path now falls through to the SPA (200 text/html, no data). Tests: the other agent's `deepstateAnalyticsProxy.test.mjs` and my `deepstateAnalyticsProxyHardening.test.mjs` tested the endpoint and were deleted with it; core coverage moved to `deepstateMirror.test.mjs` (their pure-helper tests verbatim, plus fallback on an explicit date, negative cache, per-mirror pause incl. disk served during a pause, exact 60 s minimum, network errors, empty file not stored, single-flight, time budget, source labels) — a review found five gaps versus the deleted tests via mutants, all closed. Suite 3820/3820 under Node 24 (the 3 known worldOverlayAllocation failures unchanged).
- **Front map legibility: direction markers on the line, liberated areas hatched, label halo** (2026-09-24, owner asked „čo by si vylepšil na mape?" and then „začni" for proposals 1–3). **(1) Markers „✕ N" of the GS report on the contact line.** Before, each direction marker stood at its front-scene centre („30" deep in occupied land near Kostiantynivka, „23" over Myrnohrad). Now `drawPlaces()` keeps the places each direction paragraph names (`_scenePlaces`) and `placeAnchors()` moves the marker to one of them: pure `directionAnchor(places, { avoid, minKm: 8, frontKm })` in `ukraineReportPlaces.js` scores `frontKm + 0.25 × km to the mentions-weighted centroid` (a place beyond the sampling range counts as 48 km) when the line is known, otherwise the named place nearest to the centroid; places within 8 km of an already placed marker are skipped (fall-through when nothing else is left), and directions with the fewest named places choose first so a one-place direction is not pushed onto its neighbour. The line comes from DeepState: `frontDistanceKm(index, lon, lat)` in `ukraineDeepStateLayer.js` samples 8 directions at 1/2/3/5/8/12/18/24 km and returns the first radius where the side (ru / contested / other, via `sideFromPolygons`) differs from the point's own — so the occupied × ORDLO × Crimea borders never count as front; `layer.frontKm()` caches per snapshot and returns null while the layer is hidden (a hidden layer keeps a snapshot of another day because the timeline stops updating it). `main.js` passes `frontKm` lazily and calls `ukraineReport.reanchor()` on every DeepState change. The marker is drawn 22 px above its place (`REPORT_MARKER_DY`, = half icon 11 + half label 8 + layout pad 2, so on KARTA the place's own label keeps its right-hand slot); a terrain-height answer that arrives after the marker moved is discarded and re-asked. The hover card says only what the code guarantees: „značka stojí pri sídle X, jednom z miest, ktoré hlásenie pre tento smer menuje; línia kontaktu je podľa DeepState (stav k D. M. RRRR) do ~N km od neho. Nie presný bod boja", without the line sentence when the chosen place has no distance of its own, plus „posunutá … aby neprekryla značku susedného smeru" when spacing moved it. Paragraphs that name places only as „в бік X" (70 of 574 archived paragraphs, e.g. Kupiansk 24 Sep) now count too (`PLACE_TRIGGER_RE`), which also feeds the direction card. Measured on 24 Sep (archive DeepState 23 Sep): Pokrovsk → Bilytske, Kostiantynivka → Stepanivka, Sloviansk–Kramatorsk → Yampil, Kupiansk → Kurylivka, Orikhiv → Pavlivka (all ~1 km from the line), Lyman → Drobysheve (5 km, displaced), Kherson → Dniprovske (3 km); Oleksandrivka names no place and stays at its scene centre. **(2) Liberated areas no longer look like water.** Default style draws `liberated` / `liberated-recent` with a new screen-space Fabric material `OkoHatch135` (`gl_FragCoord.x - gl_FragCoord.y`, the opposite direction of the amber grey-zone hatch), blue lines at alpha 0.42 / 0.62 with near-zero fill, outline 1.0 px at 0.5 instead of 1.8 px at 0.9; both hatch materials are registered by one `hatchFabric()` helper (OkoHatch45 source unchanged in behaviour), `HatchMaterialProperty` / `hatchMaterialFor` take `direction: -1`. The legend swatch is a 135° CSS stripe; KARTA keeps its solid fill and swatch. On the public domain (mirror, occupied only) nothing changes — the mirror has no liberated polygons. **(3) Labels.** GS-report place labels have a 4 px dark halo in the default style (`placeLabelOutlinePx`, KARTA 2 px with its pill as before); base-layer labels (settlements, rivers, oblasts) 4 px fully opaque in the default style and 3 px at 0.9 on KARTA (`BASE_LABEL_OUTLINE`, applied per style in `applyStyleItem`). Moving overlapping labels apart in the default style was first blocked by an existing test and done later the same day with the owner's consent (see „Front map clarity, part 2"); the dark pill stays KARTA-only. Verified in the pane on the Pokrovsk and Kostiantynivka scenes. A read-only review (3 lenses + a skeptic per finding) confirmed 6 issues, all fixed above: card text overstating „nearest to the line", hidden DeepState still steering anchors, greedy stacking, the missing „в бік" trigger, the base-label halo leaking into KARTA, and an 18 px offset too small for the KARTA layout. A second review (2 lenses + skeptics) confirmed 3 more, also fixed: the singular „в бік населеного пункту X" (and „в районі населеного пункту X") is a trigger too (Kupiansk 23 Sep had no place); the line is used only when the DeepState snapshot day is at most 3 days older or 1 day newer than the report day (`deepstateDayFits`, `frontKm(lon, lat, { reportDay })`; closing the timeline in replay left a 4-day-old snapshot steering the live report), and the card names that snapshot day; `HatchMaterialProperty.equals` compares type and uniforms instead of identity, so Cesium can put identical hatched polygons into one ground batch (per-polygon instances had made every liberated polygon its own GroundPrimitive). New tests `src/data/ukraineMapLegibility.test.mjs`, `src/data/ukraineBaseLabelOutline.test.mjs`; existing tests unchanged. Suite 3870/3870 under Node 24 (with the changes below) (the 3 known worldOverlayAllocation failures unchanged). Not published.
- **UKRAINE event cards: compact, no empty boxes, no overlaps; namesake geocoding** (2026-09-24, owner circled empty boxes and overlapping cards on a screenshot: „oprav aj tie kartičky aj ich zobrazenie a navrhni lepšie umiestnenie. Nemusia byť také veľké"). **Causes found:** Telegram text-only posts carry a media item of kind `text`, so the card drew an empty image frame; a video without a thumbnail (ArmyInform mp4) drew an empty 34 px bar; `.oko-ukr-pin` had `z-index: 1` and cards none, so every OTHER event's pin was painted on top of cards; `placeBox` fell back to an overlapping spot when nothing was free; `recompute()` reset the layout flag, so selection and new cards were never re-laid out while the camera stood still (new cards sat at 0,0); and the geocoder put „Житомирщина: БпЛА … Нова Борова" at Borova in Kharkiv oblast (640 km off), „на Лиманку на Одещині" at Lyman, „Донецька область" at Donetsk city. **Cards now (`src/ukraineEventsLayer.js`, `style.css`):** SMALL by default (204 × ~62 px: type glyph, ≈ for approximate, place with its full name in the tooltip, short time „HH:MM UTC" today / D.M. otherwise, a media flag, 2 status lines, and a meta line with „strojový preklad" first (never ellipsized) plus the verification level) and the full card (chips, level, 6 status lines, MT, 96 px thumbnail, source) only when selected; the small card has `var(--cursor-pointer)`. No empty frame ever: `showableMedia()` drops `text` items, a video/photos without a thumbnail get a footer link („▶ video", „▣ 1 fotka / 3 fotky / 5 fotiek" — `pluralForm`), thumbnails load eagerly behind an `is-loading` stripe placeholder, a failed thumbnail removes its frame (a photo link then opens the post; the lightbox swaps a failed photo for the Telegram embed or a link and drops the arrows and counter), a failed og:image also drops the ▣ flag. Stacking inside `.oko-ukr-cards`: pins/lines 1 < chips 2 < cards 3 < hover 4 < selected 5. Placement: pure `layoutCards()` pre-places screen obstacles (`CARD_OBSTACLE_SELECTOR`: timeline, dock, title bar, top actions, left/right rail panels, KARTA islands, mobile app bar and sheet — only elements really visible, `checkVisibility`, so clean view frees the space), hides a card whose anchor is under a panel or that has no free slot (its pin stays; click opens it), and keeps the selected card visible but never under a panel (`clearOfObstacles` tries the cross product of obstacle edges in both axes); `MAX_CARDS` 8 → 6; new cards start hidden until the first layout; image errors, arriving translations and panel toggles (debounced click / keyup / pointerup / touchend in capture, window resize) request a new frame. OSINT items that are not GeoConfirmed (the DeepState Telegram channel) now read „OSINT · <source>" instead of „OSINT overené · GeoConfirmed". **Geocoder (`locateUkText` in `src/data/ukraineMedia.js`):** a place-name adjective with an explicit ending before a stem (Нова/Стара/Велика/Мала/Верхня…; „Біля" and „Новини" are not adjectives; region stems Krym/Donbas/seas exempt — „Старий Крим" is in Crimea) makes it a different place; adjectival forms of -ськ/-цьк cities („Покровське", „Луганського") count only before „напрям/район/громад/відтин/фронт"; a city stem followed by „… обл(асть)/обл." or by coordinated adjectives with plural „областей" („Вінницької та Черкаської областей") is the oblast; „-щин-" within 3 letters („Рівненщина") is not the city. Each oblast mention is classified by FORM and the preposition before it: a header „Житомирщина:" or a locative („на Житомирщині", „у Житомирській області") qualifies the place after it in the same segment (≤ 60 chars, no . ! ? ; | newline or list emoji, abbreviations „н.п./р-ні/м./обл." ignored); after the place a locative, a genitive without preposition („Новомиколаївки Запорізької області") or „(Рівненщина)" qualifies; origin/direction („з Одещини", „на Київщину", „до Київщини", „повз") never does. A qualifying oblast farther than `OBLAST_NAMESAKE_KM` = 250 km means a namesake → that oblast (approx). `UK_OBLAST_HINTS` (17 oblasts outside the gazetteer) never give a position on their own (without a place only gazetteer oblasts do, as before) — allowing it added ~350 points at oblast centroids in a replay, incl. stories about people. Replay of the real media archive (1 294 Ukrainian items) against HEAD: 70 changed, all reviewed (e.g. Lymanka → Odesa Oblast, „Донецької області" → Donetsk Oblast, „Луганського та Покровська" → Pokrovsk, „на межі Вінницької та Черкаської областей" no longer Vinnytsia city, „Нова Одеса/Брянської області/Рівненщина" → no point instead of a wrong city). The English locator (`locateIncident`, substring matching, shared with the Gulf) has the same weakness — left for a separate task. **Placement options given to the owner:** A (done) compact cards at their points; B a column along the map edge with leader lines; C points only, card on hover/click. Verified in the pane on the Kostiantynivka scene. Three read-only reviews (3 lenses + skeptics, then 2 + 2 lenses + skeptics): 19 + 11 confirmed findings, all fixed above. New tests `src/ukraineEventsCards.test.mjs`, `src/data/ukraineMediaNamesakes.test.mjs`; existing tests unchanged. Not published.
- **DeepState: mirrors only; derived contact line** (2026-09-24, owner: „len mirrory a ostatné začni. Snaž sa o prehľadnosť ako špičkové portáli"). **Mirrors only:** the consent request of 19 Sep got no answer by the deadline (= declined under the plan's own rule), so the hourly archiver job (`jobs.deepstate` in `ukraineEventsProxy.js`) and `build-ukraine-events.mjs --deepstate` no longer call the DeepState API unless `UKRAINE_DEEPSTATE=consent`, and `/api/ukraine/events/deepstate` serves the GitHub mirror (cyterat, backup lazar-bit) to every host, localhost included; with consent the old behaviour returns (archive first, mirror for missing days). The archived files (19–24 Sep) stay on disk untouched until the owner decides. The mirror has only the occupied area, so the grey zone, liberated areas, attack directions and airfields are gone locally too (legend already handles `source: mirror`). The existing test „localhost: archive first" was rewritten to the owner's new rule (mirror on localhost, archive first only with consent). Verified: localhost 24 Sep → cyterat, 21 polygons. **Contact line (`src/data/ukraineContactLine.js`):** the map had fills, hatches and dots but no single line „this is the front". The line is derived from the RU-controlled polygons (occupied, ORDLO, Crimea, Tuzla): an edge is front when, on its outer side (ring orientation), a point 0.3 km out is not RU-controlled (so occupied × ORDLO borders are skipped) and points 3 and 6 km out lie on Ukrainian land — `src/data/ukraineLand.js`, the Natural Earth 1:50m Ukraine polygon (public domain, 636 points, generated by `scripts/build-ukraine-land.mjs`) — so the Russian state border and the coast are skipped; gaps up to 3 km between front edges are bridged (jagged outlines), pieces under 12 km dropped (DeepState's polygon does not follow the state border exactly in Luhansk oblast, leaving thin strips of „land"). Measured on the 24 Sep mirror file: 6 pieces, 1 200 km — one continuous 1 046 km line from the Dnipro at Kherson to north of Kupiansk plus five border incursions in Kharkiv/Sumy oblasts — in ~35 ms (the first version took 544 ms and gave 70 fragments); the archive snapshot of 23 Sep also gives 1 200 km. Drawn by the DeepState layer above the fills (`zIndex` 10, clamped): default style 4 px `#ff4b3e` with a 1 px dark outline, KARTA 2.2 px `#b3261e`; layer state carries `contact` / `contactKm`, the timeline legend has a „línia kontaktu (odvodená z polygónov)" swatch and the KARTA legend a line item only while it draws. Honest label: derived, not a measured line. New tests `src/data/ukraineContactLine.test.mjs`. Suite 3870/3870 under Node 24 (the 3 known worldOverlayAllocation failures unchanged). Not published.
- **Front map clarity, part 2: map-focus mode, km scale, report legend, labels, English locator** (2026-09-24/25, owner: „len mirrory a ostatné začni. Snaž sa o prehľadnosť ako špičkové portáli"). **Map focus** — `body.oko-map-focus` while a front or chokepoint scene is revealed by the zoom gate (`syncMapFocus()` in `main.js`, also re-evaluated whenever the active scene changes, e.g. Hormuz → the „Persian Gulf" situation, which the gate alone never noticed): the decorative tactical HUD (classification bar, KH11/ORB corners, rulers, GSD/NIIRS) is hidden, the bottom-left coordinates stay; the command dock is dimmed to 0.42 unless hovered or focused — opacity only, the dock geometry pinned by `creditAttribution.test.mjs` is untouched. The zoom gate now measures from `camera.positionCartographic`, so it also works in 2D/Columbus (PLÁTNO), where `positionWC` is not ECEF. An automatic legend collapse on the first scene was tried and removed after review: the timeline legend is collapsed by default anyway, so it only ever overrode an explicit user choice. **Km scale** — `src/mapScaleBar.js`: only in map focus, measured on the ellipsoid at 70 % of the view height, nice steps 1/2/5 × 10ⁿ up to 110 px („500 m", „20 km", Slovak decimal comma), placed right of the HUD coordinates (above them is the left stack's reserved gap) or, without the HUD (mobile, clean view), above the highest bottom chrome in its column (timeline, dock, mobile app bar); `position: fixed` in style.css and an obstacle for event cards. **GS report legend** — a timeline row (expanded legend, only while the report layer draws): crossed swords + „N = útoky na smere (GŠ ZSU)", place colours 0 / 1–9 / 10–24 / 25+ matching `reportIntensityColor`, and „počet neuvedený" (dashed ring) so an unstated count is not read as 0. **Labels** — report-place labels are deconflicted in the default style too (markers as fixed obstacles, point offset 8 px, no bolt offsets); the existing test that locked „default = all right, visible" was changed with the owner's consent; the no-pill-in-default test stays. **DeepState chip tooltips** now name the mirror unless the source is our archive (consent mode), and the archive text no longer claims consent is pending; hovering the contact line shows a red-accented tip with the section length. **English news locator** (`locateIncident`, Ukraine gazetteer only — the Gulf keeps substring matching): whole words (plural/possessive allowed), place-name adjectives before an alias (Nova/Stara/Velyka…) skip it, and an oblast (`UKRAINE_OBLASTS_EN`) decides a namesake only when it really qualifies the place — a header right before it („Zhytomyr region: …", „In the X region, …") or „in/of (the) X region" / „(X Oblast)" right after it, never across a sentence, dash or line break, never demoting an oblast centre (Kyiv, Kharkiv…). Replay of the English archive (1 920 news/media texts): 5 changes, all corrections — „Dnipropetrovsk region" no longer the city Dnipro, „Dniprovskyi district" and „St. Pokrovsky Church in Kyiv" → Kyiv. A read-only review (3 lenses + skeptics) of this batch and of the contact line / mirrors-only change confirmed 10 issues, all fixed above. New tests `src/mapScaleBar.test.mjs`, `src/data/ukraineIncidentsStrict.test.mjs` (plus additions); existing tests unchanged except the one label test. Suite 3880/3880 under Node 24 (the 3 known worldOverlayAllocation failures unchanged). **Published 2026-09-25 to oko.uhrin.digital (build of f190008, with all Ukraine work since d1a9392: direction card, DeepState mirror layer, thinner band, contact line, markers on the front, compact cards, map focus).**
- **ALERTS: threatened oblasts from Air Force posts** (2026-09-26, owner: „ano" to „poplachy ako vyfarbené oblasti"). New opt-in layer of the UKRAJINA module, chip POPLACHY in the panel and in the timeline row 3 (off by default, like ŠKODY). **Source:** the Air Force of Ukraine Telegram channel (`t.me/s/kpszsu`) that the media archiver already stores — no new fetch. `mediaToAlert` / `alertTargets` (`src/data/ukraineMedia.js`) turn a post into a list of threatened places in text order: oblasts of the gazetteer and of `UK_OBLAST_HINTS`, gazetteer settlements, and ~90 smaller towns the Air Force names often (`ALERT_PLACE_OBLASTS`: Vasylkiv, Slavutych, Brovary, Vyshhorod, Boyarka, Nizhyn, Voznesensk, Ochakiv, Zatoka, Tatarbunary, Nova Sanzhary, Korosten, Zvyahel… — only unambiguous names, mapped straight to their oblast); the origin of a threat is not a target („з Брянська", „з Одещини на Вінниччину" → Vinnytsia only; „на півночі від Черкас" is a position, not an origin), all-clear posts („Відбій"), impact summaries („ЗБИТО/ПОДАВЛЕНО … атакував") and posts without a threat word are skipped; „засобів ураження" (means of destruction) is not an impact. Replay of the archive (1 244 kpszsu posts, 11–26 Sep): 1 200 yield a target, the 44 without are unnamed places (Kozyn, Chabany, Ozerne…), reservoirs and „для прифронтових областей". Side effect on the event geocoder: the Lyman stem no longer matches Лиманка (Odesa oblast), `Сум`/`Білої Церкви` bare genitives are recognised — replay of 1 619 Ukrainian texts: 8 changes, all corrections (one existing test expectation is affected, see below). `assembleAlerts` in `ukraineEventsClient.js` builds the alert stream from the RAW media items of the window (not from events — `attachMedia` merges media into other events), the store returns it as `alerts` next to `events`. **Model** `src/data/ukraineAlertAreas.js` (pure): a settlement is assigned to an oblast by point-in-polygon over `public/data/ukraine-oblasts.json` (Natural Earth 1:10m admin-1, public domain, 25 polygons, simplified 0.01°, 70 KB, `scripts/build-ukraine-oblasts.mjs`; coastal cities up to 3 km outside the simplified ring still count; Russian cities fall in no oblast and are dropped); intensity per oblast from the age of its LAST post at the cursor time — 1.0 under 60 min, then steps 0.75/0.5/0.25 until 180 min, 0 after; posts after the cursor never count (replay). **Layer** `src/ukraineAlertAreasLayer.js`: one clamped polygon + outline per ring in violet `#b07cff` (fill alpha 0.3 × level, outline 0.9 × level, `zIndex` 5 so DeepState fills and the contact line stay on top), only oblasts whose step changed are rebuilt; hover: oblast, „last report N min ago", counts (last hour / 3 h), the post excerpt and the honesty line; Cesium credit „Air Force of Ukraine (t.me/kpszsu) · Natural Earth" only while something is drawn; the oblast file is fetched lazily on the first switch-on. The timeline calls `alerts.setAlerts(result.alerts, state.cursor)` on every load (LIVE = now, replay = cursor) and shows „ohrozené oblasti: N · posledné hlásenie pred M min" or „no threat in the last 3 h"; the panel chip opens the timeline when needed because the timeline is the data source. **Honesty:** this is a reported threat, explicitly NOT the official air-raid siren map (sirens are declared by regional administrations and OKO has no such feed) — said in the chip tooltip (SK/EN), legend note, hover card and DATA_SOURCES.md. Objects (oblasts), never people. New tests `src/data/ukraineAlertAreas.test.mjs`, `src/ukraineAlertAreasLayer.test.mjs`. Verified in the pane on 26 Sep (LIVE, 24 h window): 205 Air Force posts, 8 oblasts shaded, „ohrozené oblasti: 8 · posledné hlásenie pred 21 min", 20 entities. Suite 3974 tests, 3971 pass under Node 24 (the 3 known worldOverlayAllocation failures unchanged). The Lyman stem no longer matches „Лиманка" (a different settlement near Odesa); the one existing test that used that pair to exercise the oblast-qualifier rule (`ukraineMediaNamesakes.test.mjs`) was changed with the owner's „áno" to „Лиман на Одещині" (a real Lyman namesake in Odesa oblast), so the rule is still tested, and the alert extractor drops namesakes through the same `qualifyingOblasts` helper the geocoder uses. Published 2026-09-26 (build of a clean HEAD worktree, so the other agent's uncommitted account work stays out of the bundle).
- **Account launcher moved into the globe actions; sign-in polished** (2026-09-26, owner: „daj to na lepšie miesto, kde to nezavadzá, a vylepši celé prihlásenie na úroveň tohto portálu, t. j. profi"). The account centre itself (`src/auth/`, see the entry at the top of this file and `docs/AUTHENTICATION.md`) was built by another agent on the `codex/auth-profiles` branch and was still uncommitted; this commit carries that baseline plus the rework, because the two could no longer be separated file by file. **Launcher:** the 145–200 px pill (top centre right of the globe actions, and a centred bar over the map between 701 and 1 100 px — the thing the owner circled) is now the fifth 36 px glass circle inside `#top-center-actions` (`initAuthPanel` appends its `nav#account-actions` there; `panel.css` positions it `relative` in the flex group, with a fixed fallback if the group is missing). Guest: a neutral user silhouette (inline SVG — no icon font on the CSP-strict `/account.html`); signed in: the avatar symbol/photo with a green dot (amber when the backend is unreachable). Name and state stay in the DOM as visually hidden text for assistive tech and the QA gate. It hides together with the group in clean view, cockpit, scene playback, recording and clean KARTA (the previous version stayed visible there on purpose — the owner's „nezavadzá" wins). Mobile: the group gap drops to 4 px so five circles fit between the logo and SK · EN at 375 px; under 375 px the launcher sits below the language switch. **Menu:** a signed-in click opens a compact `role=menu` under the launcher (avatar, name, e-mail, owner badge; Prehľad / Profil / Zabezpečenie / Zariadenia / Aktivita / Odhlásiť sa) with ArrowUp/Down, Home/End, Escape (focus back to the launcher), Tab and outside-click closing; an item opens the centre on that page (`open(page)`). Guests still get the sign-in dialog directly. **Dialog:** on ≥ 760 px the guest view is two columns — a „Váš účet v OKO" aside with three honest bullets (profile across devices, sessions/devices you can sign out, own-data export; „the globe stays open without an account") and the form; inline errors under the field with `aria-invalid` (cleared on input) next to the status line; a Caps Lock hint on every password field; a 4-step password-strength meter (`src/auth/ux.js`: 0 under 15 characters, then length / character classes / word count, monotone passwords capped at 1) shown for registration, reset and the new password; a spinner on busy primary buttons; „Pokračovať bez účtu"; e-mail focused on open. All Slovak account copy now uses the formal address (the baseline mixed „Tvoj účet" in `strings.js` with „Vitajte" in `i18nStrings.js`); EN unchanged. The offline browser gate `scripts/qa-auth.mjs` was adapted to the new launcher (overlap = a sibling circle, hidden-with-chrome modes expect it hidden, `open()` goes through the menu when signed in) and gained two features (menu keyboard flow; inline error + meter); its hard-coded Slovak expectations follow the formal address now. Gate result: 68/68 checks, no page errors or non-fixture requests (`output/auth-qa/run-MGA9uE`).
